Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the relativity object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 600. The moduli space has dimension at most 803 with 1 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the relativity space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 600 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 803. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the relativity structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 14. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 75 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by relativity generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 14, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 75. QED.

QED.
